          ))}
      </scrollbox>

      <box
        style={{
          flexShrink: 0,
          backgroundColor: 'transparent',
        }}
      >
        {showOnboardingPrompts && !reviewMode && !freebuffControlsOpen && (
          <SuggestedPrompts
            onSelect={handleSelectSuggestedPrompt}
            maxItems={isCompactHeight ? 2 : undefined}
          />
        )}

        {/* ONE SLOT, ONE AD: a sponsored proposal replaces the display ad for
            as long as it holds the dock (`dockProposal`). */}
        {dockProposal && (
          <SponsoredProposalBlock
            key={`sponsored-dock-${dockProposal.target}`}
            block={dockProposal}
            availableWidth={separatorWidth}
            run={dockRun}
          />
        )}

        {/* Giving way to an end-of-answer card in view: the dock's rows stay,
            blank at its collapsed height, and the held ad comes back when
            the card leaves the view. */}
        {!dockProposal && ads?.[0] && showInlineAds && dockYielded && (
          <DockBlankRows />
        )}

        {!dockProposal && ads?.[0] && showInlineAds && !dockYielded && (
          <SingleAdBanner
            ad={ads[0]}
            onClick={recordClick}
            onImpression={recordImpression}
            arm={dockPanel.arm}
            // A takeover screen owns the keyboard and the space above the
            // composer, so the panel is force-closed for its duration rather
            // than fighting it for Escape.
            expanded={dockPanel.expanded && !dockTakeoverActive}
            chordHint={DOCK_CHORD_HINT}
            panelRows={dockPanelRowBudget(terminalHeight)}
            onToggle={() => dockPanel.toggle('click')}
            onClose={() => dockPanel.collapse('close')}
            // One click, one event: the dock's metadata rides the click
            // acknowledgement so the canonical server-side `ads.clicked`
            // carries it, rather than a second client-side event beside it.
            onDockClick={(clickedAd, from) =>
              recordClick(clickedAd, dockPanel.clickContext(from))
            }
          />
        )}

        {/* The partner row sits BELOW the rotating card and above the input,
            in the advertiser's own colour — it is a piece of our chrome next
            to the feature it is about, not a second banner, so it does not
            take the card's slot. Renders nothing unless the deal is live and
            the slot filled. Aligned to the card's margins above it. */}
        {showComposerPartnerAd && composerIntentAd && (
          <box style={{ marginLeft: 1, marginRight: 1 }}>
            {/* Keyed by impression: one deal's row never carries over to
                another's answer. */}
            <PartnerAdRow
              key={composerIntentAd.impUrl}
              ad={composerIntentAd}
              width={separatorWidth}
            />
          </box>
        )}

        {/* Beside the ad surfaces above, never in place of one. */}
        {IS_FREEBUFF && <ProfileSurveyBox width={separatorWidth} />}

        {IS_FREEBUFF &&
          !freebuffControlsOpen &&
          freebuffAdmissionNotice(freebuffSession) && (
            <text style={{ fg: theme.secondary, wrapMode: 'word' }}>
              {freebuffAdmissionNotice(freebuffSession)}
            </text>
          )}
        {inputMode === 'help' && !freebuffControlsOpen && <HelpBanner />}

        {shouldShowStatusLine && (
          <StatusBar
            timerStartTime={timerStartTime}
            isAtBottom={isAtBottom}
            scrollToLatest={scrollToLatest}
            statusIndicatorState={statusIndicatorState}
            onStop={chatKeyboardHandlers.onInterruptStream}
            onEndSession={() => {
              setMessages((prev) => [
                ...prev,
                getSystemMessage(END_SESSION_MESSAGE),
              ])
              returnToFreebuffLanding().catch(() => {})
            }}
            freebuffSession={freebuffSession}
          />
        )}

        {adReportPrompt ? (
          <AdReportPrompt key={adReportPrompt.entry.id} prompt={adReportPrompt} />
        ) : freebuffControlsOpen && !askUserState ? (
          <FreebuffChatControls />
        ) : reviewMode ? (
          // Review and ask_user take precedence over the session-ended banner:
          // during the grace window the agent may still be asking to run tools
          // or asking the user a question, and those approvals/answers must be
          // reachable for the run to finish — otherwise the agent hangs
          // waiting for input that can never be given.
          <ReviewScreen
            onSelectOption={handleReviewOptionSelect}
            onCustom={handleReviewCustom}
            onCancel={handleCloseReviewScreen}
          />
        ) : queuePanelOpen && !askUserState ? (
          <QueuePanel
            queuedMessages={queuedMessages}
            onEdit={editQueuedMessage}
            onDelete={removeQueuedMessage}
            onMove={moveQueuedMessage}
            onClose={handleCloseQueuePanel}
            width={separatorWidth}
            maxVisibleRows={isCompactHeight ? 4 : 8}
          />
        ) : todoPanelOpen && !askUserState && !sponsoredProposalMenuOpen ? (
          <TodoPanel
            messages={messages}
            onClose={handleCloseTodoPanel}
            maxVisibleRows={isCompactHeight ? 4 : 8}
          />
        ) : (
          <>
            <ChatInputBar
              inputValue={inputValue}
              cursorPosition={cursorPosition}
              setInputValue={setInputValue}
              inputFocused={inputFocused}
              inputRef={inputRef}
              inputPlaceholder={inputPlaceholder}
              lastEditDueToNav={lastEditDueToNav}
              agentMode={agentMode}
              toggleAgentMode={toggleAgentMode}
              setAgentMode={setAgentMode}
              hasSlashSuggestions={hasSlashSuggestions}
              hasMentionSuggestions={hasMentionSuggestions}
              hasSuggestionMenu={hasSuggestionMenu}
              slashSuggestionItems={slashSuggestionItems}
              agentSuggestionItems={agentSuggestionItems}
              fileSuggestionItems={fileSuggestionItems}
              slashSelectedIndex={slashSelectedIndex}
              agentSelectedIndex={agentSelectedIndex}
              onSlashItemClick={handleSlashItemClick}
              onMentionItemClick={handleMentionItemClick}
              theme={theme}
              terminalHeight={terminalHeight}
              separatorWidth={separatorWidth}
              shouldCenterInputVertically={shouldCenterInputVertically}
              inputBoxTitle={inputBoxTitle}
              onQueuePreviewClick={openQueuePanel}
              isCompactHeight={isCompactHeight}
              isNarrowWidth={isNarrowWidth}
              feedbackMode={feedbackMode}
              handleExitFeedback={handleExitFeedback}
              publishMode={publishMode}
              handleExitPublish={handleExitPublish}
              handlePublish={handlePublish}
              handleSubmit={handleSubmit}
              onPaste={withPasteCount(createPasteHandler({
                text: inputValue,
                cursorPosition,
                onChange: setInputValue,
                onPasteImage: chatKeyboardHandlers.onPasteImage,
                onPasteImagePath: chatKeyboardHandlers.onPasteImagePath,
                onPasteFilePath: chatKeyboardHandlers.onPasteFilePath,
                onPasteLongText: (pastedText) => {
                  const id = crypto.randomUUID()
                  const preview = pastedText.slice(0, 100).replace(/\n/g, ' ')
                  useChatStore.getState().addPendingTextAttachment({
                    id,
                    content: pastedText,
                    preview,
                    charCount: pastedText.length,
                  })
                  // Show temporary status message
                  showClipboardMessage(
                    `📋 Pasted text (${pastedText.length.toLocaleString()} chars)`,
                    { durationMs: 5000 },
                  )
                },
                cwd: getProjectRoot() ?? process.cwd(),
              }))}
              onInterruptStream={chatKeyboardHandlers.onInterruptStream}
            />
          </>
        )}
        {IS_FREEBUFF && !hasSelectedByokConnection && <FreebuffChatFooter projectRoot={getProjectRoot()} />}
      </box>
    </box>
  )
}
```
- File `cli/src/components/ad-banner.tsx` (1049 lines):
```
import { TextAttributes } from '@opentui/core'
import {
  DOCK_LABEL_SEPARATOR,
  DOCK_PANEL_MAX_WIDTH,
  DOCK_SPONSORED_LABEL,
  INLINE_AD_DISCLOSURE,
  INLINE_AD_GAP,
  INLINE_AD_LINK_SUFFIX,
  MAX_DESC_LINES,
  getAdDisplayLabel,
  getDockAdLayout,
  getDockPanelLayout,
  getInlineAdLayout,
  truncateToLines,
  truncateToWidth,
} from '@codebuff/common/ads/inline-ad-layout'
import type { DockClickOrigin } from '@codebuff/common/ads/ad-event-hygiene'
import type { CliDockArm } from '@codebuff/common/util/ad-experiment'
import { visibleWaitingRoomPlacementIds } from '@codebuff/common/ads/waiting-room-placements'
import { isRemoteSession, showClipboardMessage } from '../utils/clipboard'
import { isOpenableWebUrl, safeOpen } from '../utils/open-url'
import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react'

import { greptileTerminalColors } from '../ads/partner-brand'
import { layoutTruncated } from '../ads/ad-engagement'
import {
  reportInlineCardDrawn,
  reportInlineCardVisibility,
} from '../ads/ad-screen'
import { useAdEngagement } from '../ads/use-ad-engagement'

import { Button } from './button'
import { AdReportButton, AD_REPORT_CONTROL_COLUMNS } from './ad-report-button'
import { useTerminalDimensions } from '../hooks/use-terminal-dimensions'
import { useTheme } from '../hooks/use-theme'
import { BORDER_CHARS, INVERTED_CTA_FG } from '../utils/ui-constants'

import type { AdResponse } from '../hooks/use-gravity-ad'

interface ChoiceAdBannerProps {
  ads: AdResponse[]
  placementIds?: readonly string[]
  onClick?: (ad: AdResponse) => void
  onImpression?: (ad: AdResponse) => void
}

// border-top + 2 copy rows + cta row + border-bottom. The two copy rows are
// headline + 1 description line, or 2 description lines when the ad has no
// headline — see getCardAdLayout. Fixed either way, because the landing screen
// subtracts this from the model picker's height budget.
export const AD_CARD_HEIGHT = 5
export const INLINE_AD_CARD_HEIGHT = 4 // border-top + header row + detail row + border-bottom

// Layout lives in `common` so the advertiser campaign builder's creative
// preview fits copy exactly the way this renderer does. Re-exported here
// because this module was its original home.
export {
  extractDomain,
  getAdDisplayLabel,
  getDockAdLayout,
  getDockPanelLayout,
  getInlineAdLayout,
} from '@codebuff/common/ads/inline-ad-layout'

export function getCardAdLayout(
  ad: Pick<AdResponse, 'adText' | 'title' | 'cta' | 'url'>,
  width: number,
): {
  headline: string
  description: string
  descriptionLines: number
  ctaText: string
  labelText: string
  labelVariant: 'domain' | 'title'
} {
  // Every field is defaulted before it is read. `AdResponse` types these as
  // required strings, but nothing enforces that at runtime: the Gravity
  // provider casts `response.json()` rather than parsing it and `normalize()`
  // copies `cta: raw.cta` with no default — so a missing field is a case this
  // codebase already expects from a network. A throw here is a throw
  // inside AdCard's render on the landing screen, and `error-boundary.tsx` is a
  // passthrough that does not catch render errors.
  const title = (ad.title ?? '').trim()
  const cta = (ad.cta ?? '').trim()
  const adText = ad.adText ?? ''
  const url = ad.url ?? ''

  // Interior width less the padding and the ' Ad' disclosure, matching what
  // the description has always been given.
  const copyWidth = Math.max(0, width - 8)
  const headline = truncateToWidth(title, copyWidth)
  const descriptionLines = headline ? 1 : MAX_DESC_LINES
  // The title is no longer a CTA fallback: it has a row of its own, and using
  // it here too printed the same string twice on a five-row card.
  const ctaText = cta || 'Learn more'
  // Called with the defaulted fields, not `ad`: it reads `ad.url.trim()`
  // directly and would throw on the same malformed payload.
  const label = getAdDisplayLabel({ title, url })
  // Without a URL the label falls back to the title, which is now drawn one row
  // above. Same string, twice, for the same reason.
  const showLabel = label.variant === 'domain' || !headline

  return {
    headline,
    description: truncateToLines(adText, copyWidth, descriptionLines),
    descriptionLines,
    ctaText,
    labelText: showLabel
      ? truncateToWidth(label.text, Math.max(0, width - ctaText.length - 5))
      : '',
    labelVariant: label.variant,
  }
}

/** How long the "copy this link" line stays up after an ad click cannot open. */
export const AD_LINK_NOTICE_MS = 15_000

/** What became of one click on an ad. */
export type AdClickOutcome = 'opened' | 'link_shown' | 'ignored'

export type AdClickDeps = {
  open: (url: string) => Promise<boolean>
  isRemote: () => boolean
  showLink: (ad: Pick<AdResponse, 'url'>) => void
}

/**
 * The line shown when a click cannot open a browser here. It carries the
 * advertiser's plain landing `url`, never the tracked `clickUrl`: the tracked
 * link is a short-lived, per-impression redirect that counts a click when it
 * is followed, so pasting it elsewhere would count a click this client chose
 * not to record, and it stays live in scrollback after the user moves on.
 */
export function adLinkNotice(ad: Pick<AdResponse, 'url'>): string {
  return isOpenableWebUrl(ad.url)
    ? `Couldn't open a browser here. Link: ${ad.url}`
    : "Couldn't open a browser here."
}

const liveAdClickDeps: AdClickDeps = {
  open: safeOpen,
  isRemote: isRemoteSession,
  showLink: (ad) =>
    showClipboardMessage(adLinkNotice(ad), { durationMs: AD_LINK_NOTICE_MS }),
}

/**
 * The one path every ad click in this file takes: try to open the tracked
 * link, and report the click (`onOpened`) only when a browser was opened.
 *
 * A session over SSH never opens: the opener would run on the remote host,
 * where nobody is looking, and report success. There, and whenever opening
 * fails (no display server, WSL without Windows interop, a refused URL), no
 * click is reported and the landing URL is shown as a line to copy instead.
 */
export async function openAdClick(
  ad: Pick<AdResponse, 'clickUrl' | 'url'>,
  onOpened: () => void,
  deps: AdClickDeps = liveAdClickDeps,
): Promise<AdClickOutcome> {
  if (!ad.clickUrl) return 'ignored'
  let opened = false
  if (!deps.isRemote()) {
    try {
      opened = await deps.open(ad.clickUrl)
    } catch {
      opened = false
    }
  }
  if (opened) {
    onOpened()
    return 'opened'
  }
  deps.showLink(ad)
  return 'link_shown'
}

/**
 * Calculate evenly distributed column widths that sum exactly to availableWidth.
 * Distributes remainder pixels across the first N columns so there's no gap.
 */
function columnWidths(count: number, availableWidth: number): number[] {
  const base = Math.floor(availableWidth / count)
  const remainder = availableWidth - base * count
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => base + (i < remainder ? 1 : 0))
}

/**
 * A single ad card: full-width above the input ({@link SingleAdBanner}),
 * content-width when interspersed inside an assistant response
 * (BlocksRenderer), and in a row of columns on the landing screen
 * ({@link ChoiceAdBanner}). Manages its own hover state.
 *
 * Impressions are deduped per impUrl in the ads hook. A pinned card (dock,
 * landing screen) reports its impression on mount and on ad rotation. An
 * inline transcript card reports it only once its rows have stayed inside the
 * transcript viewport for the dwell (`useAdEngagement`'s `onSeen`), or on a
 * click, whichever comes first.
 */
export const AdCard: React.FC<{
  ad: AdResponse
  width: number
  variant?: 'card' | 'inline'
  onClick?: (ad: AdResponse) => void
  onImpression?: (ad: AdResponse) => void
}> = ({ ad, width, variant = 'card', onClick, onImpression }) => {
  const baseTheme = useTheme()
  const brand = greptileTerminalColors(ad)
  const theme = brand
    ? { ...baseTheme, foreground: brand.ink, muted: brand.ink, primary: brand.ink }
    : baseTheme
  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false)

  const isInline = variant === 'inline'
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isInline) return
    onImpression?.(ad)
  }, [ad, onImpression, isInline])
  // One ad on screen at a time: our own units read whether a card is drawn.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isInline) return
    const drawn = { impUrl: ad.impUrl, provider: ad.provider }
    reportInlineCardDrawn(drawn, true)
    return () => reportInlineCardDrawn(drawn, false)
  }, [ad.impUrl, ad.provider, isInline])

  // Laid out before the early return below, so the engagement hook can judge
  // truncation from exactly what is drawn.
  const inlineLayout =
    variant === 'inline' ? getInlineAdLayout(ad, width) : null
  if (inlineLayout) {
    inlineLayout.title = truncateToWidth(
      inlineLayout.title,
      Math.max(0, width - 8 - AD_REPORT_CONTROL_COLUMNS),
    )
  }
  const card = variant === 'inline' ? null : getCardAdLayout(ad, width)
  if (card?.headline) {
    card.headline = truncateToWidth(
      card.headline,
      Math.max(0, width - 8 - AD_REPORT_CONTROL_COLUMNS),
    )
  }
  // An inline card scrolls with the transcript and is measured; the dock and
  // the landing screen's columns sit outside any scrollbox.
  const engagement = useAdEngagement(ad.impUrl, {
    placement: variant === 'inline' ? 'measured' : 'pinned',
    truncated: inlineLayout
      ? layoutTruncated([
          [
            (ad.title ?? '').trim() ||
              getAdDisplayLabel({ title: '', url: ad.url ?? '' }).text,
            inlineLayout.title,
          ],
          [ad.adText, inlineLayout.description],
        ])
      : card
        ? layoutTruncated([
            [ad.title, card.headline],
            [ad.adText, card.description],
          ])
        : undefined,
    ...(isInline
      ? {
          onSeen: () => onImpression?.(ad),
          // One ad on screen at a time: the dock gives way while this card
          // is in the transcript viewport.
          onVisibility: (visible: boolean | undefined) =>
            reportInlineCardVisibility(ad, visible),
        }
      : {}),
  })

  const buttonProps = {
    ref: engagement.ref,
    onClick: (event?: unknown) => {
      if (!ad.clickUrl) return
      engagement.onClick(event)
      // A click is proof the card was on screen: an inline card clicked
      // before its dwell elapsed reports its impression now (deduped).
      onImpression?.(ad)
      void openAdClick(ad, () => onClick?.(ad))
    },
    onMouseOver: () => {
      setIsHovered(true)
      engagement.onHover(true)
    },
    onMouseOut: () => {
      setIsHovered(false)
      engagement.onHover(false)
    },
  }

  if (inlineLayout) {
    const accentColor = isHovered ? theme.primary : theme.muted
    return (
      <Button
        {...buttonProps}
        style={{
          width,
          height: INLINE_AD_CARD_HEIGHT,
          backgroundColor: brand?.background,
          borderStyle: 'single',
          borderColor: accentColor,
          customBorderChars: BORDER_CHARS,
          paddingLeft: 1,
          paddingRight: 1,
          flexDirection: 'column',
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}
      >
        <box
          style={{
            width: '100%',
            height: 1,
            flexDirection: 'row',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            overflow: 'hidden',
          }}
        >
          <text
            style={{
              fg: isHovered ? theme.primary : theme.foreground,
              flexShrink: 1,
              wrapMode: 'none',
            }}
            attributes={TextAttributes.BOLD}
          >
            {inlineLayout.title}
          </text>
          <box style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexShrink: 0 }}>
            <text style={{ fg: theme.muted, wrapMode: 'none' }}>
              {INLINE_AD_DISCLOSURE}
            </text>
            <AdReportButton ad={{ ...ad, surface: 'cli' }} color={theme.muted} />
          </box>
        </box>
        <box
          style={{
            width: '100%',
            height: 1,
            flexDirection: 'row',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            columnGap: INLINE_AD_GAP,
            overflow: 'hidden',
          }}
        >
          <text style={{ fg: theme.muted, flexShrink: 1, wrapMode: 'none' }}>
            {inlineLayout.description}
          </text>
          {inlineLayout.label && (
            <text
              style={{
                fg: accentColor,
                flexShrink: 0,
                wrapMode: 'none',
              }}
              attributes={TextAttributes.UNDERLINE}
            >
              {inlineLayout.label + INLINE_AD_LINK_SUFFIX}
            </text>
          )}
        </box>
      </Button>
    )
  }

  if (!card) return null

  return (
    <Button
      {...buttonProps}
      style={{
        width,
        height: AD_CARD_HEIGHT,
        backgroundColor: brand?.background,
        borderStyle: 'single',
        borderColor: isHovered ? theme.primary : theme.muted,
        customBorderChars: BORDER_CHARS,
        paddingLeft: 1,
        paddingRight: 1,
        flexDirection: 'column',
      }}
    >
      {/* The disclosure rides whichever row is first, so it is never below the
          fold of a card whose body has shrunk to one line. */}
      {card.headline ? (
        <box
          style={{
            flexDirection: 'row',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            alignItems: 'flex-start',
            height: 1,
            overflow: 'hidden',
          }}
        >
          <text
            style={{
              fg: isHovered ? theme.primary : theme.foreground,
              flexShrink: 1,
              wrapMode: 'none',
            }}
            attributes={TextAttributes.BOLD}
          >
            {card.headline}
          </text>
          <box style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexShrink: 0 }}>
            <text style={{ fg: theme.muted }}>{' Ad'}</text>
            <AdReportButton ad={{ ...ad, surface: 'cli' }} color={theme.muted} />
          </box>
        </box>
      ) : null}
      <box
        style={{
          flexDirection: 'row',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          alignItems: 'flex-start',
          height: card.descriptionLines,
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}
      >
        <text style={{ fg: theme.muted, flexShrink: 1 }}>
          {card.description}
        </text>
        {card.headline ? null : (
          <box style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexShrink: 0 }}>
            <text style={{ fg: theme.muted }}>{' Ad'}</text>
            <AdReportButton ad={{ ...ad, surface: 'cli' }} color={theme.muted} />
          </box>
        )}
      </box>
      <box style={{ flexGrow: 1 }} />
      {/* Bottom: CTA + domain */}
      <box
        style={{
          flexDirection: 'row',
          columnGap: 1,
          alignItems: 'center',
          height: 1,
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}
      >
        <text
          style={{
            fg: brand?.background ?? INVERTED_CTA_FG,
            bg: isHovered ? theme.primary : theme.muted,
            attributes: TextAttributes.BOLD,
          }}
        >
          {` ${card.ctaText} `}
        </text>
        {card.labelText ? (
          <text
            style={{
              fg: theme.muted,
              wrapMode: 'none',
              attributes:
                card.labelVariant === 'domain'
                  ? TextAttributes.UNDERLINE
                  : TextAttributes.BOLD,
            }}
          >
            {card.labelText}
          </text>
        ) : null}
      </box>
    </Button>
  )
}

/**
 * The V2 resting dock (COD-457): `Sponsored · Brand` on the label row, headline
 * and description on the left, a bordered CTA box on the right.
 *
 * Still exactly {@link AD_CARD_HEIGHT} rows, because the landing screen
 * subtracts that from the model picker's budget. Below
 * `MIN_INLINE_WIDTH_WITH_DESTINATION` this delegates to {@link AdCard}
 * verbatim — narrow terminals keep the layout the house-ad width budget was
 * measured against, and expansion still works there.
 *
 * Nothing here depends on hover. Hover tints the border and inverts the CTA
 * box, exactly as the card always did; expansion is a click or the chord.
 */
export const DockAdCard: React.FC<{
  ad: AdResponse
  width: number
  expanded?: boolean
  chordHint?: string
  onToggle?: () => void
  onClick?: (ad: AdResponse, from: DockClickOrigin) => void
  onImpression?: (ad: AdResponse) => void
}> = ({
  ad,
  width,
  expanded = false,
  chordHint,
  onToggle,
  onClick,
  onImpression,
}) => {
  const baseTheme = useTheme()
  const brand = greptileTerminalColors(ad)
  const theme = brand
    ? { ...baseTheme, foreground: brand.ink, muted: brand.ink, primary: brand.ink }
    : baseTheme
  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false)
  const [isCtaHovered, setIsCtaHovered] = useState(false)

  const layout = useMemo(
    () => getDockAdLayout(ad, width, { chordHint, collapsed: !expanded }),
    [ad, width, chordHint, expanded],
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    if (layout.mode !== 'dock') return
    onImpression?.(ad)
  }, [ad, onImpression, layout.mode])

  // The narrow fallback renders `AdCard`, which tracks itself.
  const engagement = useAdEngagement(ad.impUrl, {
    placement: 'pinned',
    enabled: layout.mode === 'dock',
    truncated:
      layout.mode === 'dock'
        ? layoutTruncated([
            [ad.title, layout.headline],
            [ad.adText, layout.description],
            [(ad.cta ?? '').trim() || 'Learn more', layout.ctaText],
          ])
        : undefined,
  })

  // Hover is read from the tint state: the children's over/out bubble to the
  // box and React batches an out-then-over into no change at all.
  const { onHover: reportHover } = engagement
  useEffect(() => {
    reportHover(isHovered)
  }, [isHovered, reportHover])

  if (layout.mode === 'card') {
    // The narrow fallback keeps today's card AND today's click semantics: the
    // whole card opens the landing page, and the dock's toggle is reached from
    // the chord instead. Two different meanings for one click at 40 columns
    // would be worse than no expansion affordance at all.
    return (
      <AdCard
        ad={ad}
        width={width}
        onClick={(clicked) => onClick?.(clicked, 'dock')}
        onImpression={onImpression}
      />
    )
  }

  const accentColor = isHovered ? theme.primary : theme.muted

  return (
    // A plain box, NOT a Button. The CTA used to be a Button nested inside the
    // dock's own Button, and OpenTUI mouse events propagate: one press on the
    // CTA opened the advertiser's URL *and* toggled the panel, recording a
    // false expansion. Rather than rely on stopping propagation through the
    // shared Button, the two clickable regions are siblings and never nest.
    // Hover still tints the whole border, because mouse events bubble UP to
    // this box from either child.
    <box
      onMouseOver={() => setIsHovered(true)}
      onMouseOut={() => setIsHovered(false)}
      style={{
        width,
        height: AD_CARD_HEIGHT,
        backgroundColor: brand?.background,
        borderStyle: 'single',
        borderColor: accentColor,
        customBorderChars: BORDER_CHARS,
        paddingLeft: 1,
        paddingRight: 1,
        flexDirection: 'row',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }}
    >
      {/* The copy block is the toggle. */}
      <Button
        onClick={() => onToggle?.()}
        style={{
          width: layout.copyWidth,
          flexDirection: 'column',
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}
      >
        <box
          style={{
            height: 1,
            flexDirection: 'row',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            overflow: 'hidden',
          }}
        >
          <box style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexShrink: 1 }}>
            <text style={{ fg: theme.primary, wrapMode: 'none' }}>
              {layout.sponsored}
            </text>
            <text style={{ fg: theme.muted, wrapMode: 'none' }}>
              {DOCK_LABEL_SEPARATOR}
            </text>
            <text
              style={{
                fg: theme.foreground,
                flexShrink: 1,
                wrapMode: 'none',
                attributes:
                  layout.brandVariant === 'domain'
                    ? TextAttributes.UNDERLINE
                    : undefined,
              }}
            >
              {layout.brand}
            </text>
          </box>
          {layout.chordHint ? (
            <text style={{ fg: theme.muted, flexShrink: 0, wrapMode: 'none' }}>
              {layout.chordHint}
            </text>
          ) : null}
          <AdReportButton ad={{ ...ad, surface: 'cli' }} color={theme.muted} />
        </box>
        {layout.headline ? (
          <text
            style={{ fg: theme.foreground, height: 1, wrapMode: 'none' }}
            attributes={TextAttributes.BOLD}
          >
            {layout.headline}
          </text>
        ) : null}
        <text
          style={{ fg: theme.muted, height: layout.descriptionLines }}
        >
          {layout.description}
        </text>
      </Button>
      <box style={{ flexGrow: 1 }} />
      {/* A SIBLING of the toggle, never a child: the CTA opens the landing
          page and must not also expand the panel. */}
      <Button
        onClick={(event?: unknown) => {
          if (!ad.clickUrl) return
          engagement.onClick(event, 'cta')
          void openAdClick(ad, () => onClick?.(ad, 'dock'))
        }}
        onMouseOver={() => setIsCtaHovered(true)}
        onMouseOut={() => setIsCtaHovered(false)}
        style={{
          width: layout.ctaBoxWidth,
          height: 3,
          borderStyle: 'single',
          borderColor: theme.primary,
          customBorderChars: BORDER_CHARS,
          paddingLeft: 1,
          paddingRight: 1,
          flexShrink: 0,
        }}
      >
        <text
          style={{
            fg: isCtaHovered ? brand?.background ?? INVERTED_CTA_FG : theme.primary,
            bg: isCtaHovered ? theme.primary : undefined,
            wrapMode: 'none',
          }}
          attributes={TextAttributes.BOLD}
        >
          {layout.ctaText + INLINE_AD_LINK_SUFFIX}
        </text>
      </Button>
    </box>
  )
}

/**
 * The expanded detail panel (COD-457).
 *
 * Rendered IN FLOW directly above the dock rather than as an absolute overlay
 * — see the PR: nothing else in the CLI positions absolutely, and a panel that
 * mispaints over the transcript is worse than one that shortens it.
 *
 * Returns null when {@link getDockPanelLayout} says the smallest panel would
 * still not fit: a panel that covers the composer is never drawn.
 */
export const DockDetailPanel: React.FC<{
  ad: AdResponse
  width: number
  availableRows: number
  onClose: () => void
  onClick?: (ad: AdResponse, from: DockClickOrigin) => void
}> = ({ ad, width, availableRows, onClose, onClick }) => {
  const baseTheme = useTheme()
  const brand = greptileTerminalColors(ad)
  const theme = brand
    ? { ...baseTheme, foreground: brand.ink, muted: brand.ink, primary: brand.ink }
    : baseTheme
  const [isCtaHovered, setIsCtaHovered] = useState(false)
  const [isCloseHovered, setIsCloseHovered] = useState(false)

  const panel = useMemo(
    () => getDockPanelLayout(ad, { width, availableRows }),
    [ad, width, availableRows],
  )

  // A second drawn copy of the dock's impression: hovering or clicking the
  // panel is engagement with the same ad, on the same record.
  const engagement = useAdEngagement(ad.impUrl, {
    placement: 'pinned',
    enabled: panel.fits,
  })

  if (!panel.fits) return null

  const interiorWidth = Math.max(0, panel.width - 4)

  return (
    <box
      onMouseOver={() => engagement.onHover(true)}
      onMouseOut={() => engagement.onHover(false)}
      style={{
        width: panel.width,
        height: panel.height,
        backgroundColor: brand?.background,
        borderStyle: 'single',
        borderColor: theme.primary,
        customBorderChars: BORDER_CHARS,
        paddingLeft: 1,
        paddingRight: 1,
        flexDirection: 'column',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }}
    >
      <box
        style={{
          height: 1,
          flexDirection: 'row',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }}
      >
        <text
          style={{ fg: theme.primary, flexShrink: 1, wrapMode: 'none' }}
          attributes={TextAttributes.BOLD}
        >
          {panel.brand}
        </text>
        <box style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexShrink: 0 }}>
          <text style={{ fg: theme.primary, wrapMode: 'none' }}>
            {DOCK_SPONSORED_LABEL}
          </text>
          <AdReportButton ad={{ ...ad, surface: 'cli' }} color={theme.muted} />
        </box>
      </box>
      <box style={{ height: 1 }} />
      <text
        style={{ fg: theme.foreground, height: 1, wrapMode: 'none' }}
        attributes={TextAttributes.BOLD}
      >
        {panel.headline}
      </text>
      {panel.bodyLines.map((line, index) => (
        <text
          key={`body-${index}`}
          style={{ fg: theme.muted, height: 1, wrapMode: 'none' }}
        >
          {line}
        </text>
      ))}
      {panel.diagram ? (
        <>
          <box style={{ height: 1 }} />
          <text style={{ fg: theme.muted, height: 1, wrapMode: 'none' }}>
            {panel.diagram}
          </text>
        </>
      ) : null}
      {panel.bullets.length > 0 ? (
        <>
          <box style={{ height: 1 }} />
          {panel.bullets.map((bullet, index) => (
            <text
              key={`bullet-${index}`}
              style={{ fg: theme.muted, height: 1, wrapMode: 'none' }}
            >
              {`• ${bullet}`}
            </text>
          ))}
        </>
      ) : null}
      <text style={{ fg: theme.muted, height: 1, wrapMode: 'none' }}>
        {'─'.repeat(interiorWidth)}
      </text>
      <box style={{ height: 1 }} />
      <box style={{ height: 3, flexDirection: 'row', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        <Button
          onClick={(event?: unknown) => {
            if (!ad.clickUrl) return
            engagement.onClick(event, 'cta')
            void openAdClick(ad, () => onClick?.(ad, 'panel'))
          }}
          onMouseOver={() => setIsCtaHovered(true)}
          onMouseOut={() => setIsCtaHovered(false)}
          style={{
            height: 3,
            borderStyle: 'single',
            borderColor: theme.primary,
            customBorderChars: BORDER_CHARS,
            paddingLeft: 1,
            paddingRight: 1,
            flexShrink: 0,
          }}
        >
          <text
            style={{
              fg: isCtaHovered ? brand?.background ?? INVERTED_CTA_FG : theme.primary,
              bg: isCtaHovered ? theme.primary : undefined,
              wrapMode: 'none',
            }}
            attributes={TextAttributes.BOLD}
          >
            {panel.ctaText + INLINE_AD_LINK_SUFFIX}
          </text>
        </Button>
        <box style={{ flexGrow: 1 }} />
        <box style={{ flexDirection: 'column', flexShrink: 0 }}>
          <box style={{ height: 1 }} />
          <Button
            onClick={onClose}
            onMouseOver={() => setIsCloseHovered(true)}
            onMouseOut={() => setIsCloseHovered(false)}
            style={{ height: 1 }}
          >
            <text
              style={{
                fg: isCloseHovered ? theme.foreground : theme.muted,
                wrapMode: 'none',
              }}
            >
              {DOCK_CLOSE_LABEL}
            </text>
          </Button>
        </box>
      </box>
    </box>
  )
}

/** The panel's own close control. Exported so the tmux capture can assert it. */
export const DOCK_CLOSE_LABEL = '[ Close ]'

/**
 * Rows the composer and its surrounding chrome are never allowed to lose.
 *
 * The dock itself is {@link AD_CARD_HEIGHT}; the rest is the bordered input
 * box plus the hint row beneath it. Deliberately generous: overestimating
 * costs a bullet row, and underestimating covers the input box, which is the
 * one outcome the spec forbids outright.
 */
export const DOCK_COMPOSER_RESERVED_ROWS = AD_CARD_HEIGHT + 6

/**
 * Rows the dock keeps, blank, while it gives way to an end-of-answer card:
 * the collapsed dock's own height at every width and arm (the detail panel
 * collapses when the dock goes blank), so nothing above or below it moves
 * and a resize cannot leave it stale.
 */
export const DOCK_BLANK_ROWS = AD_CARD_HEIGHT

/** The dock's rows while it gives way: blank, its collapsed height. */
export const DockBlankRows: React.FC = () => (
  <box style={{ height: DOCK_BLANK_ROWS, flexShrink: 0 }} />
)

/**
 * How many rows the panel may occupy above the dock at this terminal height.
 *
 * Pure and tested, for the same reason the degradation ladder is: "never
 * covers the composer at 24 rows" cannot be seen in a snapshot of a tall
 * terminal.
 */
export function dockPanelRowBudget(terminalHeight: number): number {
  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(terminalHeight) - DOCK_COMPOSER_RESERVED_ROWS)
}

/**
 * The rotating ad pinned above the chat input box. Rerenders (and fires a new
 * impression) each time the hook rotates `ads[0]`.
 *
 * `arm` is the sticky CLI dock arm (COD-457). `control` renders the card
 * exactly as it did before this issue — same component, same props, same five
 * rows — so the control arm is byte-identical to today by construction rather
 * than by a test that could drift.
 */
export const SingleAdBanner: React.FC<{
  ad: AdResponse
  onClick?: (ad: AdResponse) => void
  onImpression?: (ad: AdResponse) => void
  arm?: CliDockArm
  expanded?: boolean
  chordHint?: string
  /** Rows the panel may occupy above the dock without covering the composer. */
  panelRows?: number
  onToggle?: () => void
  onDockClick?: (ad: AdResponse, from: DockClickOrigin) => void
  onClose?: () => void
}> = ({
  ad,
  onClick,
  onImpression,
  arm = 'control',
  expanded = false,
  chordHint,
  panelRows = 0,
  onToggle,
  onDockClick,
  onClose,
}) => {
  const { terminalWidth } = useTerminalDimensions()
  const width = terminalWidth - 2

  if (arm !== 'expandable') {
    return (
      <box style={{ marginLeft: 1, marginRight: 1 }}>
        <AdCard
          ad={ad}
          width={width}
          onClick={onClick}
          onImpression={onImpression}
        />
      </box>
    )
  }

  return (
    // `flexShrink: 0` is load-bearing, not decoration. Without it the panel is
    // the only flexible thing between a transcript that will not shrink and a
    // composer that must not, so a tall panel gets its bottom border clipped
    // and paints over the dock's label row. Measured at 80x24.
    <box
      style={{
        marginLeft: 1,
        marginRight: 1,
        flexDirection: 'column',
        flexShrink: 0,
      }}
    >
      {expanded ? (
        // Anchored to the dock's right edge, per the mockup.
        <box
          style={{
            flexDirection: 'row',
            justifyContent: 'flex-end',
            flexShrink: 0,
          }}
        >
          <DockDetailPanel
            ad={ad}
            width={Math.min(DOCK_PANEL_MAX_WIDTH, width)}
            availableRows={panelRows}
            onClose={() => onClose?.()}
            onClick={onDockClick}
          />
        </box>
      ) : null}
      <DockAdCard
        ad={ad}
        width={width}
        expanded={expanded}
        chordHint={chordHint}
        onToggle={onToggle}
        onClick={onDockClick}
        onImpression={onImpression}
      />
    </box>
  )
}

/**
 * Up to four ads shown in a row. Still used by the freebuff landing screen,
 * which intentionally fills the space with multiple ads.
 */
export const ChoiceAdBanner: React.FC<ChoiceAdBannerProps> = ({
  ads,
  placementIds,
  onClick,
  onImpression,
}) => {
  const { terminalWidth } = useTerminalDimensions()

  // Available width for cards (terminal minus left/right margin of 1 each)
  const colAvail = terminalWidth - 2

  // Only show as many ads as fit with a healthy minimum width; hide the rest
  const maxVisible =
    placementIds?.length ?? visibleWaitingRoomPlacementIds(terminalWidth).length
  const visibleAds = useMemo(() => {
    const requested = placementIds?.length
      ? orderedRequestedAds(ads, placementIds)
      : ads
    return requested.slice(0, maxVisible)
  }, [ads, maxVisible, placementIds])

  const widths = useMemo(
    () => columnWidths(visibleAds.length, colAvail),
    [visibleAds.length, colAvail],
  )

  return (
    <box
      style={{
        width: '100%',
        flexDirection: 'column',
      }}
    >
      {/* Card columns */}
      <box
        style={{
          marginLeft: 1,
          marginRight: 1,
          flexDirection: 'row',
        }}
      >
        {visibleAds.map((ad, i) => (
          <AdCard
            key={ad.impUrl}
            ad={ad}
            width={widths[i]}
            onClick={onClick}
            onImpression={onImpression}
          />
        ))}
      </box>
    </box>
  )
}

/** Preserve canonical request order and never mount a duplicate slot response. */
export function orderedRequestedAds(
  ads: AdResponse[],
  placementIds: readonly string[],
): AdResponse[] {
  return placementIds.flatMap((placementId) => {
    const ad = ads.find((candidate) => candidate.placementId === placementId)
    return ad ? [ad] : []
  })
}

- File `cli/src/components/display-ad-splash.tsx` (276 lines):

/**
 * `cliSplash`: the mock sponsor block under the launch header box.
 *
 * Boxed (the default): a 1px sponsor-colour border round an inverse band
 * (lettermark, bold name, "Sponsored") over a tinted body with the headline,
 * the body line, a filled "domain ↗" chip and "hide for today". The one-line
 * variant is an inverse "Sponsored │ Name" chip, the tagline and the domain,
 * for short terminals. Both carry the one disclosure and a hide control.
 * The header's own bottom margin spaces the block from the transcript.
 *
 * MOCK (the local `cliSplash` flag): `sponsorFor('cliSplash')` from common's
 * fixture, nothing fetched or billed; the impression (on insertion), the CTA
 * and "hide for today" go to analytics only (`ads/display-ads-telemetry.ts`).
 *
 * SERVED (flag off, `CLI-Splash` in the policy's `displayPlacementIds`): one
 * served ad (`ads/served-display-ads.ts`) drawn through the same views, with
 * the billed impression on insertion and the billed click before opening its
 * clickUrl. No fill draws nothing. "Hide for today" stays local.
 */
import { sponsorFor } from '@codebuff/common/ads/display-ads/sponsors'
import { TextAttributes } from '@opentui/core'

import { Button } from './button'
import { useNetworkAdOnScreen } from '../ads/ad-screen'
import {
  CLI_DISPLAY_AD_PLACEMENT_IDS,
  cliDisplayAdEnabled,
  cliSponsorColors,
} from '../ads/display-ads'
import {
  useDisplayAdsStore,
  useSplashHiddenToday,
} from '../ads/display-ads-store'
import {
  trackCliDisplayAd,
  useCliDisplayAdImpression,
} from '../ads/display-ads-telemetry'
import {
  recordServedDisplayClick,
  useServedDisplayAd,
  useServedDisplayImpression,
  type GetDisplayAd,
} from '../ads/served-display-ads'
import { useTheme } from '../hooks/use-theme'
import { safeOpen } from '../utils/open-url'

import type { CliSponsorColors } from '../ads/display-ads'
import type { DisplaySponsor } from '@codebuff/common/ads/display-ads/served'

export type DisplayAdSplashVariant = 'boxed' | 'line'

/** Rows below which the launch header gets the one-line variant. */
export const SPLASH_BOXED_MIN_ROWS = 28

export function displayAdSplashVariant(
  terminalHeight: number,
): DisplayAdSplashVariant {
  return terminalHeight < SPLASH_BOXED_MIN_ROWS ? 'line' : 'boxed'
}

/** The flagged or served, not-hidden-today block; renders nothing otherwise. */
export function DisplayAdSplash({
  width,
  variant,
  placementId = CLI_DISPLAY_AD_PLACEMENT_IDS.cliSplash,
  enabled = cliDisplayAdEnabled('cliSplash'),
  getServed,
  open = safeOpen,
}: {
  width: number
  variant: DisplayAdSplashVariant
  placementId?: string
  /** Test seam; production reads the settings flag. */
  enabled?: boolean
  /** Test seam for the served fetch. */
  getServed?: GetDisplayAd
  open?: (url: string) => unknown
}) {
  const theme = useTheme()
  const userHidden = useSplashHiddenToday()
  // One ad on screen at a time: our unit gives way (draws and requests
  // nothing) while an ad-network ad is on screen in the chat.
  const networkAdOnScreen = useNetworkAdOnScreen()
  const hidden = userHidden || networkAdOnScreen
  const hide = useDisplayAdsStore((s) => s.hideSplashForToday)
  const mock = enabled ? sponsorFor('cliSplash') : null
  const served = useServedDisplayAd(placementId, !enabled && !hidden, getServed)
  const shownMock = enabled && !hidden && Boolean(mock)
  useCliDisplayAdImpression('cliSplash', mock?.id, shownMock)
  // Inactive (flag on, or hidden today) the served hook holds and asks nothing.
  useServedDisplayImpression(served)
  const sponsor: DisplaySponsor | null = shownMock ? mock : served
  if (!sponsor) return null
  const colors = cliSponsorColors(sponsor.theme, theme)
  const onOpen = () => {
    if (served) recordServedDisplayClick(served)
    else if (mock)
      trackCliDisplayAd(
        { slot: 'cliSplash', sponsorId: mock.id, kind: 'click', action: 'cta' },
        enabled,
      )
    void open(sponsor.url)
  }
  const onHide = () => {
    if (mock)
      trackCliDisplayAd(
        {
          slot: 'cliSplash',
          sponsorId: mock.id,
          kind: 'dismiss',
          action: 'hide_button',
          scope: 'day',
        },
        enabled,
      )
    hide()
  }
  return variant === 'line' ? (
    <DisplayAdSplashLine
      sponsor={sponsor}
      colors={colors}
      width={width}
      placementId={placementId}
      muted={theme.muted}
      onOpen={onOpen}
      onHide={onHide}
    />
  ) : (
    <DisplayAdSplashBoxed
      sponsor={sponsor}
      colors={colors}
      width={width}
      placementId={placementId}
      onOpen={onOpen}
      onHide={onHide}
    />
  )
}

interface ViewProps {
  sponsor: DisplaySponsor
  colors: CliSponsorColors
  width: number
  placementId: string
  onOpen: () => void
  onHide: () => void
}

export function DisplayAdSplashBoxed({
  sponsor,
  colors,
  width,
  placementId,
  onOpen,
  onHide,
}: ViewProps) {
  return (
    <box
      id={placementId}
      style={{
        width,
        marginTop: 1,
        border: true,
        borderColor: colors.border,
        flexDirection: 'column',
        backgroundColor: colors.tint,
      }}
    >
      <Button
        onClick={onOpen}
        style={{
          width: '100%',
          height: 1,
          paddingLeft: 1,
          paddingRight: 1,
          flexDirection: 'row',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          backgroundColor: colors.fill,
        }}
      >
        <text style={{ fg: colors.ink, wrapMode: 'none', flexShrink: 1 }}>
          <span bg={colors.ink} fg={colors.fill}>
            <b>{` ${sponsor.lettermark} `}</b>
          </span>
          <b>{` ${sponsor.name}`}</b>
        </text>
        <text style={{ fg: colors.ink, wrapMode: 'none', flexShrink: 0 }}>
          Sponsored
        </text>
      </Button>
      <box
        style={{
          flexDirection: 'column',
          paddingLeft: 1,
          paddingRight: 1,
          paddingTop: 1,
        }}
      >
        <text
          style={{ fg: colors.tintText, wrapMode: 'word' }}
          attributes={TextAttributes.BOLD}
        >
          {sponsor.copy.headline}
        </text>
        <text style={{ fg: colors.tintMuted, wrapMode: 'word' }}>
          {sponsor.copy.body}
        </text>
        <box
          style={{
            flexDirection: 'row',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            marginTop: 1,
          }}
        >
          <Button onClick={onOpen} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }}>
            <text style={{ wrapMode: 'none' }}>
              <span bg={colors.fill} fg={colors.ink}>
                <b>{` ${sponsor.domain} ↗ `}</b>
              </span>
            </text>
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={onHide} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }}>

- File `cli/src/components/display-ad-statusline.tsx` (188 lines):
```
/**
 * `cliStatusline`: the mock sponsor row at the bottom of the Freebuff footer.
 *
 * One full-width row in the sponsor's fill with its ink: an inverse
 * "Ad │ Name" chip (ink background, fill text), the tagline, a "/deals" hint
 * and × to hide it for this session (`/deals off` does the same). As the row
 * narrows it gives up the tagline, then the hint; the chip and × always draw.
 *
 * MOCK (the local `cliStatusline` flag): `sponsorFor('cliStatusline')` from
 * common's fixture; the impression (on insertion), the row click and × go to
 * analytics only (`ads/display-ads-telemetry.ts`).
 *
 * SERVED (flag off, `CLI-Statusline` in the policy's `displayPlacementIds`):
 * one served ad (`ads/served-display-ads.ts`) in the same row, billed
 * impression on insertion, billed click before opening its clickUrl. No fill
 * draws nothing. × stays local (this session only).
 */
import { sponsorFor } from '@codebuff/common/ads/display-ads/sponsors'
import { useRef } from 'react'

import { Button } from './button'
import {
  CLI_DISPLAY_AD_PLACEMENT_IDS,
  cliDisplayAdEnabled,
  cliSponsorColors,
} from '../ads/display-ads'
import { useNetworkAdOnScreen } from '../ads/ad-screen'
import { useDisplayAdsStore } from '../ads/display-ads-store'
import {
  trackCliDisplayAd,
  useCliDisplayAdImpression,
} from '../ads/display-ads-telemetry'
import {
  recordServedDisplayClick,
  useServedDisplayAd,
  useServedDisplayImpression,
  type GetDisplayAd,
} from '../ads/served-display-ads'
import { getSessionDisplayPlacementIds } from '../hooks/use-dock-panel'
import { useTheme } from '../hooks/use-theme'
import { safeOpen } from '../utils/open-url'

import type { CliSponsorColors } from '../ads/display-ads'
import type { DisplaySponsor } from '@codebuff/common/ads/display-ads/served'

export const DEALS_HINT = '/deals'

export function DisplayAdStatusline({
  placementId = CLI_DISPLAY_AD_PLACEMENT_IDS.cliStatusline,
  enabled = cliDisplayAdEnabled('cliStatusline'),
  getServed,
  open = safeOpen,
  dealsLive,
}: {
  /** Test seam: whether CLI-Deals is announced (else read off the policy). */
  dealsLive?: boolean
  placementId?: string
  /** Test seam; production reads the settings flag. */
  enabled?: boolean
  /** Test seam for the served fetch. */
  getServed?: GetDisplayAd
  open?: (url: string) => unknown
}) {
  const theme = useTheme()
  const userHidden = useDisplayAdsStore((s) => s.statuslineHidden)
  // One ad on screen at a time: our unit gives way (draws and requests
  // nothing) while an ad-network ad is on screen in the chat.
  const networkAdOnScreen = useNetworkAdOnScreen()
  const hidden = userHidden || networkAdOnScreen
  const setHidden = useDisplayAdsStore((s) => s.setStatuslineHidden)
  const mock = enabled ? sponsorFor('cliStatusline') : null
  // Inactive (flag on, or hidden) the served hook holds and asks nothing.
  const served = useServedDisplayAd(placementId, !enabled && !hidden, getServed)
  const shownMock = enabled && !hidden && Boolean(mock)
  useCliDisplayAdImpression('cliStatusline', mock?.id, shownMock)
  useServedDisplayImpression(served)
  const sponsor: DisplaySponsor | null = shownMock ? mock : served
  // A row once drawn keeps its one row, blank, while it gives way to an
  // ad-network ad, so the transcript above does not move each time the dock
  // changes supplier. Hiding it (×, /deals off) gives the row back.
  const drewRef = useRef(false)
  if (userHidden) drewRef.current = false
  if (!sponsor) {
    return networkAdOnScreen && drewRef.current ? (
      <box style={{ height: 1, flexShrink: 0 }} />
    ) : null
  }
  drewRef.current = true
  return (
    <DisplayAdStatuslineView
      sponsor={sponsor}
      colors={cliSponsorColors(sponsor.theme, theme)}
      placementId={placementId}
      // The hint points at /deals: a served row shows it only when CLI-Deals
      // is served too, else /deals would answer "No deals are available."
      showDealsHint={
        shownMock ||
        (dealsLive ??
          (getSessionDisplayPlacementIds() ?? []).includes(
            CLI_DISPLAY_AD_PLACEMENT_IDS.deals,
          ))
      }
      onOpen={() => {
        if (served) recordServedDisplayClick(served)
        else if (mock)
          trackCliDisplayAd(
            {
              slot: 'cliStatusline',
              sponsorId: mock.id,
              kind: 'click',
              action: 'row',
            },
            enabled,
          )
        void open(sponsor.url)
      }}
      onHide={() => {
        if (mock)
          trackCliDisplayAd(
            {
              slot: 'cliStatusline',
              sponsorId: mock.id,
              kind: 'dismiss',
              action: 'hide_button',
              scope: 'session',
            },
            enabled,
          )
        setHidden(true)
      }}
    />
  )
}

export function DisplayAdStatuslineView({
  sponsor,
  colors,
  placementId,
  showDealsHint = true,
  onOpen,
  onHide,
}: {
  showDealsHint?: boolean
  sponsor: DisplaySponsor
  colors: CliSponsorColors
  placementId: string
  onOpen: () => void
  onHide: () => void
}) {
  return (
    <box
      id={placementId}
      style={{
        width: '100%',
        height: 1,
        flexDirection: 'row',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        backgroundColor: colors.fill,
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }}
    >
      <Button
        onClick={onOpen}
        style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexShrink: 1, overflow: 'hidden' }}
      >
        <text style={{ wrapMode: 'none', flexShrink: 0 }}>
          <span bg={colors.ink} fg={colors.fill}>
            <b>{` Ad │ ${sponsor.name} `}</b>
          </span>
        </text>
        <text style={{ fg: colors.ink, wrapMode: 'none', flexShrink: 1 }}>
          {` ${sponsor.copy.tagline}`}
        </text>
      </Button>
      <box style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexShrink: 0 }}>
        {showDealsHint && (
          <text style={{ fg: colors.ink, wrapMode: 'none' }}>
            <b>{` ${DEALS_HINT} `}</b>
          </text>
        )}
        <Button onClick={onHide} style={{ paddingLeft: 1, paddingRight: 1 }}>
          <text style={{ fg: colors.ink, wrapMode: 'none' }}>×</text>
        </Button>
      </box>
    </box>
  )
}

describe('one ad on screen at a time: a held or superseded ad', () => {
  test('a dock ad held while a card has the screen keeps one record and counts the hidden time', () => {
    const { c, sent, registry } = registryHarness()
    const first = registry.mount('a', 'pinned')!
    c.advance(10_000)
    // the dock draws a blank in its place: the card unmounts, then the slot holds it
    registry.unmount(first)
    registry.hold('a', 'inline_card_in_view')
    c.advance(30_000)
    // well past the release grace: nothing was flushed
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(0)
    const again = registry.mount('a', 'pinned')!
    expect(again.tracker).toBe(first.tracker)
    registry.release('a')
    c.advance(5_000)
    registry.unmount(again)
    c.advance(ENGAGEMENT_RELEASE_GRACE_MS)
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(sent[0]).toMatchObject({
      impUrl: 'a',
      exit: 'unmount',
      visibleMs: 15_000,
      yielded: { reason: 'inline_card_in_view', count: 1, hiddenMs: 30_000 },
    })
  })
  test('a hold placed before the unmount works the same way', () => {
    const { c, sent, registry } = registryHarness()
    const first = registry.mount('a', 'pinned')!
    registry.hold('a', 'inline_card_in_view')
    registry.unmount(first)
    c.advance(5_000)
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(0)
    registry.closeAll()
    expect(sent[0]).toMatchObject({
      exit: 'window_close',
      yielded: { reason: 'inline_card_in_view', count: 1, hiddenMs: 5_000 },
    })
  })
  test('a held ad released without a redraw (it rotated away while hidden) ends after the grace', () => {
    const { c, sent, registry } = registryHarness()
    const first = registry.mount('a', 'pinned')!
    registry.unmount(first)
    registry.hold('a', 'inline_card_in_view')
    c.advance(2_000)
    registry.noteSlotSwap(['a'], ['b'])
    registry.release('a')
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(0)
    c.advance(ENGAGEMENT_RELEASE_GRACE_MS)

      impUrl: 'a',
      exit: 'ro

- File `cli/src/ads/__tests__/use-composer-intent.test.ts`: "the composer row gate closing"; "a draft waiting for its idle timer is never asked about"; "a late answer is not shown; the same draft on reopen shows it with no new request"
```
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test'
import { createComposerIntentScheduler } from '@codebuff/common/ads/composer-intent-scheduler'
/**
 * What `useComposerIntentAd` relies on when its gate closes (an ad-network
 * ad on screen, a menu, a form): it tells the scheduler the draft is empty.
 */
describe('the composer row gate closing', () => {
  const harness = () => {
    const timers: Array<() => void> = []
    const answers: Array<(ad: string | null) => void> = []
    const asked: string[] = []
    const shown: Array<string | null> = []
    const scheduler = createComposerIntentScheduler<string>({
      request: (draft) => {
        asked.push(draft)
        return new Promise((resolve) => answers.push(resolve))
      },
      onAnswer: (ad) => shown.push(ad),
      setTimeout: (fn) => {
        timers.push(fn)
        return timers.length
      },
      clearTimeout: () => {
        timers.length = 0
      },
    })
    const flush = async () => {
      await Promise.resolve()
      await Promise.resolve()
    }
    return { scheduler, timers, answers, asked, shown, flush }
  }
  test('a draft waiting for its idle timer is never asked about', () => {
    const h = harness()
    h.scheduler.update('open a pull request')
    h.scheduler.update('') // gate closed before the idle timer fired
    for (const fire of h.timers.splice(0)) fire()
    expect(h.asked).toEqual([])
    expect(h.shown).toEqual([null])
  })
  test('a late answer is not shown; the same draft on reopen shows it with no new request', async () => {
    const h = harness()
    h.scheduler.update('open a pull request')
    h.timers.shift()!()
    expect(h.asked).toEqual(['open a pull request'])
    h.scheduler.update('') // gate closed while the request is out
    h.answers.shift()!('row')
    await h.flush()
    expect(h.shown).toEqual([null])
    h.scheduler.update('open a pull request') // gate reopened, same draft
    h.timers.shift()!()
    expect(h.asked).toEqual(['open a pull request
```

  *   seen, or after {@link ENGAGEMENT_CHECKPOINT_MS} of exposure, whichever
  *   comes first;
  * - once, at the first click, because the click is the label the record
  *   exists for.
  *
  * LATE CLICKS. The dock redraws a cached creative under its original
  * `impUrl` long after that impression's record went out, and the server
  * still bills the click. A mount of an already-flushed `impUrl` therefore
  * gets a CLICK-ONLY tracker: it measures nothing about the exposure (that
  * record is closed) and sends only `{ v, impUrl, click }` -- plus `postClick`
  * -- as a merge record. When the impression was already clicked, the first
  * click's features are kept and only the count grows, so a late click never
  * overwrites the click it follows.
  */

+/**
+ * ONE AD ON SCREEN AT A TIME: what the chat's two main ad units hold and
+ * show, so every other unit can give way.
+ *
+ * The two main units are the slot above the input (the dock) and the
+ * end-of-answer card in the transcript. The rules:
+ *
+ * 1. A PAIR FROM TWO SUPPLIERS: the ad network always wins.
+ *    - network dock + network card in view: the dock goes blank (the card
+ *      outranks it);
+ *    - network dock + our card: OUR CARD is not drawn
+ *      (`suppressOurCards`), and the card request carries
+ *      `networkAdInView` so the server answers with the network or nothing;
+ *    - our dock + network card in view: our dock goes blank;
+ *    - our dock + our card in view: the dock goes blank.
+ *    A blank dock requests nothing, pauses its rotation clock and shows the
+ *    ad it held when the card leaves the view.
+ * 2. Only the newest answer's card is drawn (`keepNewestResponseAd`).
+ * 3. Our own units (partner rows, display units) neither show nor request
+ *    while an ad-network ad is HELD by the dock (drawn or blank) or DRAWN as
+ *    the card (`networkAdOnScreen`). Held and drawn, not "visible right now":
+ *    the dock going blank for a card never changes the gate, so a row
+ *    appearing cannot move the card and flip the dock back.
+ * 4. A sponsored offer held in the dock is never hidden; the card request
+ *    for the answer waits until the offer has gone.
+ *
+ * IN VIEW, WITH HYSTERESIS. The card counts as in view from the first
+ * measurement inside the viewport, and stops only after it has been
+ * measured outside it (or unmeasurable) for {@link CARD_OUT_OF_VIEW_RELEASE_MS}
+ * without a break. A card that cannot be measured never makes the dock go
+ * blank, so an unreadable layout cannot leave the dock blank for a session.
+ */
+import { create } from 'zustand'
+
+import { TRANSCRIPT_IMPRESSION_DWELL_MS } from './ad-engagement'
+
+import type { AdProvider, AdResponse } from '../hooks/use-gravity-ad'
+
+export type OnScreenAd = { impUrl: string; provider?: AdProvider }
+
+/** How long the card must stay out of view before the dock may come back. */
+export const CARD_OUT_OF_VIEW_RELEASE_MS = TRANSCRIPT_IMPRESSION_DWELL_MS
+
+interface AdScreenState {
+  /** The end-of-answer card in the transcript viewport (with hysteresis). */
+  inlineCard: OnScreenAd | null
+  /** The end-of-answer card drawn in the transcript, in view or not. */
+  inlineCardDrawn: OnScreenAd | null
+  /** The ad the dock holds, drawn or blank; null with no ad or an offer. */
+  dockHeld: OnScreenAd | null
+}
+
+export const useAdScreenStore = create<AdScreenState>(() => ({
+  inlineCard: null,
+  inlineCardDrawn: null,
+  dockHeld: null,
+}))
+
+/** When the current in-view card was first measured out of view. */
+let outOfViewSince: number | null = null
+
+/**
+ * An ad from an ad network rather than our own inventory. A missing provider
+ * counts as a network ad, so our own units give way when in doubt.
+ */
+export function isNetworkAd(
+  ad: Pick<OnScreenAd, 'provider'> | null | undefined,
+): boolean {
+  return ad != null && ad.provider !== 'first_party'
+}
+
+function isOurAd(ad: Pick<OnScreenAd, 'provider'> | null | undefined): boolean {
+  return ad != null && ad.provider === 'first_party'
+}
+
+export function networkAdOnScreen(
+  state: Pick<AdScreenState, 'dockHeld' | 'inlineCardDrawn'>,
+): boolean {
+  return isNetworkAd(state.dockHeld) || isNetworkAd(state.inlineCardDrawn)
+}
+
+/** Whether our own units must give way now (rule 3). */
+export function useNetworkAdOnScreen(): boolean {
+  return useAdScreenStore(networkAdOnScreen)
+}
+
+/** The end-of-answer card in the transcript viewport, if any (rule 1). */
+export function useInlineCardOnScreen(): OnScreenAd | null {
+  return useAdScreenStore((s) => s.inlineCard)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Whether an end-of-answer card request must carry `networkAdInView`: the
+ * dock holds an ad-network ad, so a card of ours would not be drawn.
+ */
+export function cardRequestNeedsNetworkHint(): boolean {
+  return isNetworkAd(useAdScreenStore.getState().dockHeld)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Whether a dock request must carry `networkAdInView`: an ad-network card
+ * is in view, so a dock ad of ours could not be drawn.
+ */
+export function dockRequestNeedsNetworkHint(): boolean {
+  return isNetworkAd(useAdScreenStore.getState().inlineCard)
+}
+
+/**
+ * A fill that came back for a request sent with `networkAdInView` and is
+ * ours anyway (a server that ignores the hint): dropped, never drawn.
+ */
+export function dropOurFillBesideNetwork(
+  ad: Pick<OnScreenAd, 'provider'> | null | undefined,
+  hintSent: boolean,
+): boolean {
+  return hintSent && isOurAd(ad)
+}
+
+/**
+ * The cards drawable beside what the dock holds: with an ad-network ad in
+ * the dock, our own cards are left out (rule 1). Returns `cards` itself
+ * when nothing is left out, so its identity stays stable.
+ */
+export function suppressOurCards<T extends Pick<OnScreenAd, 'provider'>>(
+  cards: Record<string, T[]>,
+  dockHeld: Pick<OnScreenAd, 'provider'> | null | undefined,
+): Record<string, T[]> {
+  if (!isNetworkAd(dockHeld)) return cards
+  let changed = false
+  const next: Record<string, T[]> = {}
+  for (const [id, list] of Object.entries(cards)) {
+    const kept = list.filter((ad) => !isOurAd(ad))
+    if (kept.length !== list.length) changed = true
+    if (kept.length > 0) next[id] = kept
+  }
+  return changed ? next : cards
+}
+
+/**
+ * The card's latest viewport measurement. In view (`true`) holds the screen
+ * at once; out of view or unmeasurable (`false` / `undefined`) releases it
+ * only once that has lasted {@link CARD_OUT_OF_VIEW_RELEASE_MS}. A report
+ * for another card never clears the current one.
+ */
+export function reportInlineCardVisibility(
+  ad: Pick<AdResponse, 'impUrl' | 'provider'>,
+  visible: boolean | undefined,
+  now: number = Date.now(),
+): void {
+  const current = useAdScreenStore.getState().inlineCard
+  if (visible === true) {
+    if (current?.impUrl === ad.impUrl) outOfViewSince = null
+    if (current?.impUrl === ad.impUrl && current.provider === ad.provider)
+      return
+    outOfViewSince = null
+    useAdScreenStore.setState({
+      inlineCard: { impUrl: ad.impUrl, provider: ad.provider },
+    })
+    return
+  }
+  if (current?.impUrl !== ad.impUrl) return
+  if (outOfViewSince === null) {
+    outOfViewSince = now
+    return
+  }
+  if (now - outOfViewSince >= CARD_OUT_OF_VIEW_RELEASE_MS) {
+    outOfViewSince = null
+    useAdScreenStore.setState({ inlineCard: null })
+  }
+}
+
+/** The card mounted (`true`) or went away (`false`, which also ends "in view"). */
+export function reportInlineCardDrawn(
+  ad: Pick<AdResponse, 'impUrl' | 'provider'>,
+  drawn: boolean,
+): void {
+  const state = useAdScreenStore.getState()
+  if (drawn) {
+    if (
+      state.inlineCardDrawn?.impUrl === ad.impUrl &&
+      state.inlineCardDrawn.provider === ad.provider
+    )
+      return
+    useAdScreenStore.setState({
+      inlineCardDrawn: { impUrl: ad.impUrl, provider: ad.provider },
+    })
+    return
+  }
+  const patch: Partial<AdScreenState> = {}
+  if (state.inlineCardDrawn?.impUrl === ad.impUrl) patch.inlineCardDrawn = null
+  if (state.inlineCard?.impUrl === ad.impUrl) {
+    patch.inlineCard = null
+    outOfViewSince = null
+  }
+  if (Object.keys(patch).length > 0) useAdScreenStore.setState(patch)
+}
+
+/** The ad the dock holds (drawn or blank), or null when it holds none. */
+export function reportDockHeld(
+  ad: Pick<AdResponse, 'impUrl' | 'provider'> | null | undefined,
+): void {
+  const current = useAdScreenStore.getState().dockHeld
+  if (!ad) {
+    if (current) useAdScreenStore.setState({ dockHeld: null })
+    return
+  }
+  if (current?.impUrl === ad.impUrl && current.provider === ad.provider) return
+  useAdScreenStore.setState({
+    dockHeld: { impUrl: ad.impUrl, provider: ad.provider },
+  })
+}
+
+/** Test seam. */
+export function resetAdScreenForTests(): void {
+  outOfViewSince = null
+  useAdScreenStore.setState({
+    inlineCard: null,
+    inlineCardDrawn: null,
+    dockHeld: null,
+  })
+}
+
+/**
+ * The end-of-answer cards after `messageId` received `ad`: only that answer's
+ * card is kept, unless a NEWER answer (later in `answerOrder`) already has
+ * one, or the answer is no longer in the transcript (`/new`, a clear), in
+ * which case the late card is dropped and nothing drawn changes. Returns the
+ * next map and the drawn cards that gave way.
+ */
+export function keepNewestResponseAd<T>(
+  prev: Record<string, T[]>,
+  messageId: string,
+  ad: T,
+  answerOrder: readonly string[],
+): { next: Record<string, T[]>; superseded: T[]; dropped: boolean } {
+  const incoming = answerOrder.indexOf(messageId)
+  if (incoming < 0) return { next: prev, superseded: [], dropped: true }
+  const newer = Object.keys(prev).some(
+    (id) => id !== messageId && answerOrder.indexOf(id) > incoming,
+  )
+  if (newer) return { next: prev, superseded: [], dropped: true }
+  const superseded: T[] = []
+  for (const [id, ads] of Object.entries(prev))
+    if (id !== messageId) superseded.push(...ads)
+  return {
+    next: { [messageId]: [...(prev[messageId] ?? []), ad] },
+    superseded,
+    dropped: false,
+  }
+}

  * re-ask the same draft.
  */
 export function useComposerIntentAd(
   draft: string,
   enabled: boolean,
 ): AdResponse | null {
   const [ad, setAd] = useState<AdResponse | null>(null)
   const scheduler = useRef<ComposerIntentScheduler | null>(null)
 
   useEffect(() => {
     const next = createComposerIntentScheduler<AdResponse>({
       request: (text) => getComposerIntentAd(text),
       onAnswer: setAd,
     })
     scheduler.current = next
     return () => {
       next.dispose()
       if (scheduler.current === next) scheduler.current = null
     }
   }, [])
 
   useEffect(() => {
     if (enabled) scheduler.current?.update(draft)
   }, [draft, enabled])
 
+  // Disabled (a menu, a form, or an ad-network ad on screen): a draft waiting
+  // for its idle timer is not asked about, a late answer is not shown, and
+  // the last row is forgotten, so reopening never redraws a stale row. An
+  // answer already asked for stays remembered by the scheduler and is shown
+  // again, with no new request, if the same draft is still there.
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (enabled) return
+    scheduler.current?.update('')
+    setAd(null)
+  }, [enabled])
+
   return enabled ? ad : null
 }

 /**
  * How many rows the panel may occupy above the dock at this terminal height.
  *
  * Pure and tested, for the same reason the degradation ladder is: "never
  * covers the composer at 24 rows" cannot be seen in a snapshot of a tall
  * terminal.
  */
 export function dockPanelRowBudget(terminalHeight: number): number {
   return Math.max(0, Math.floor(terminalHeight) - DOCK_COMPOSER_RESERVED_ROWS)
 }

   const stickyColumnRef = useRef<number | null>(null)
 
   // Refs to track latest value and cursor position synchronously for IME input handling.
   // When IME sends multiple character events rapidly (e.g., Chinese input), React batches
   // state updates, causing subsequent events to see stale closure values. These refs are
   // updated synchronously to ensure each keystroke builds on the previous one.
   const valueRef = useRef(value)
   const cursorPositionRef = useRef(cursorPosition)
 
   // Keep refs current on every render (synchronous assignment avoids useEffect timing issues)
   valueRef.current = value
   cursorPositionRef.current = cursorPosition
 
+  // A plain Enter submits only if it is the last key of its stdin read.
+  // OpenTUI hands every key of one read to these handlers before React
+  // re-renders, and a terminal without bracketed paste (a phone keyboard's
+  // clipboard, some web terminals) types a paste out as keys, its line breaks
+  // as Return. Submitting on each one sent the same draft once per pasted line:
+  // hundreds of identical prompts, refused as `scripted` (2026-11-11). The
+  // submit waits for a microtask, which runs after the read's last key; text
+  // typed before then turns the pending Enter into a line break.
+  const pendingSubmitRef = useRef(false)
+  const onSubmitRef = useRef(onSubmit)
+  onSubmitRef.current = onSubmit
+
   // Helper to get or set the sticky column for vertical navigation.
   // When stickyColumnRef.current is set, we return it (preserving column across
   // multiple up/down presses). When null, we calculate from current cursor position.
   const getOrSetStickyColumn = useCallback(
     (lineStarts: number[], cursorIsChar: boolean): number => {
       if (stickyColumnRef.current != null) {
         return stickyColumnRef.current
       }
       const lineIndex = lineStarts.findLastIndex(
         (lineStart) => lineStart <= cursorPosition,
       )
       const column =
         lineIndex === -1
           ? 0
           : cursorPosition - lineStarts[lineIndex] + (cursorIsChar ? -1 : 0)
       stickyColumnRef.current = Math.max(0, column)
       return stickyColumnRef.current
     },
     [cursorPosition],
   )
 
   // Update last activity on value or cursor changes
   useEffect(() => {
     setLastActivity(Date.now())
   }, [value, cursorPosition])

 /** Whether the rows `measure` reports lie in the transcript viewport. */
 export function answerEndVisible(
   measure: MeasureRows | undefined,
 ): boolean | undefined {

+/**
+ * The rotation clock after the slot gave way for `yieldedAt..now`: the
+ * hidden time does not count, so the held ad gets the rest of its interval.
+ * `firstTickInMs` is when the next rotation is due; null `lastRotationAt`
+ * (nothing shown yet) starts a fresh interval.
+ */
+export function resumeRotationAfterYield(params: {
+  lastRotationAt: number | null
+  yieldedAt: number
+  now: number
+  intervalMs?: number
+}): { lastRotationAt: number | null; firstTickInMs: number } {
+  const interval = params.intervalMs ?? AD_ROTATION_INTERVAL_MS
+  if (params.lastRotationAt === null)
+    return { lastRotationAt: null, firstTickInMs: interval }
+  const hiddenMs = Math.max(0, params.now - params.yieldedAt)
+  const shownMs = Math.max(0, params.yieldedAt - params.lastRotationAt)
+  return {
+    lastRotationAt: params.lastRotationAt + hiddenMs,
+    firstTickInMs: Math.max(0, interval - shownMs),
+  }
+}
+
+/** The dock giving way, while it does: since when, and what it holds. */
+export type SlotYieldHold = { since: number; impUrls: string[] }
+
+/**
+ * A rotation's answer that landed while the slot had given way (a fetch
+ * already in flight when the card came into view): kept, not drawn, and
+ * shown when the slot comes back, with no second request. `undefined` is
+ * "nothing held"; `null` is a held "clear the slot" (a withheld answer).
+ */
+export type HeldRotation = AdResponse[] | null | undefined
+
+/**
+ * What one rotation answer does to the slot: `show` it now, or keep it
+ * `held` because the slot has given way since the request left.
+ */
+export function deliverRotation(
+  ctrl: { heldRotation: HeldRotation },
+  show: AdResponse[] | null | undefined,
+  yielded: boolean,
+): 'show' | 'held' | 'none' {
+  if (show === undefined) return 'none'
+  if (!yielded) return 'show'
+  ctrl.heldRotation = show
+  return 'held'
+}
+
+/**
+ * The slot giving way (`yielded` true) or coming back (false). Returns the
+ * next hold. Giving way holds the drawn ads' engagement records open.
+ * Coming back, in order: a rotation answer held meanwhile is shown (a full
+ * interval from now); else a first fetch skipped meanwhile runs; else the
+ * clock resumes with the rest of the held ad's interval. The held records
+ * are released after the swap, so an ad replaced meanwhile ends as a
+ * rotation.
+ */
+export function applySlotYield(
+  hold: SlotYieldHold | null,
+  params: {
+    yielded: boolean
+    now: number
+    /** impUrls the slot draws now. */
+    drawnImpUrls: readonly string[]
+    /** The slot is started and not hidden. */
+    slotActive: boolean
+    focused: boolean | undefined
+    ctrl: {
+      lastRotationAt: number | null
+      initialFetchPending: boolean
+      heldRotation: HeldRotation
+    }
+    hold: (impUrl: string) => void
+    release: (impUrl: string) => void
+    show: (ads: AdResponse[] | null) => void
+    runInitialFetch: () => void
+    restartRotationTimer: (firstDelayMs?: number) => void
+    intervalMs?: number
+  },
+): SlotYieldHold | null {
+  if (params.yielded) {
+    if (hold) return hold
+    const impUrls = [...params.drawnImpUrls]
+    for (const impUrl of impUrls) params.hold(impUrl)
+    return { since: params.now, impUrls }
+  }
+  if (!hold) return null
+  const ctrl = params.ctrl
+  const held = ctrl.heldRotation
+  ctrl.heldRotation = undefined
+  if (params.slotActive && held !== undefined) {
+    params.show(held)
+    ctrl.lastRotationAt = params.now
+    params.restartRotationTimer()
+  } else if (
+    params.slotActive &&
+    ctrl.initialFetchPending &&
+    adFocusAllows(params.focused)
+  ) {
+    params.runInitialFetch()
+    params.restartRotationTimer()
+  } else if (params.slotActive) {
+    const resumed = resumeRotationAfterYield({
+      lastRotationAt: ctrl.lastRotationAt,
+      yieldedAt: hold.since,
+      now: params.now,
+      intervalMs: params.intervalMs,
+    })
+    ctrl.lastRotationAt = resumed.lastRotationAt
+    params.restartRotationTimer(resumed.firstTickInMs)
+  }
+  for (const impUrl of hold.impUrls) params.release(impUrl)
+  return null
+}
+
+/**
+ * The impUrls on screen for the focus-return path: the dock's ads unless the
+ * slot has given way, and every drawn end-of-answer card.
+ */
+export function drawnAdImpUrls(params: {
+  dockAds: readonly AdResponse[] | null
+  dockYielded: boolean
+  responseAds: Record<string, readonly AdResponse[]>
+}): Set<string> {
+  const drawn = new Set<string>()
+  if (!params.dockYielded)
+    for (const ad of params.dockAds ?? []) drawn.add(ad.impUrl)
+  for (const list of Object.values(params.responseAds))
+    for (const ad of list) drawn.add(ad.impUrl)
+  return drawn
+}

 type ChoiceCache = Pick<GravityController, 'choiceCache' | 'choiceCacheIndex'>
 
 // Pure helper: add an ad set to the cache
 function addToChoiceCache(ctrl: ChoiceCache, ads: AdResponse[]): void {
   // Deduplicate by checking if any set has the same first impUrl
   const key = ads[0]?.impUrl
   if (key && ctrl.choiceCache.some((set) => set[0]?.impUrl === key)) return
   if (ctrl.choiceCache.length >= MAX_AD_CACHE_SIZE) ctrl.choiceCache.shift()
   ctrl.choiceCache.push(ads)
 }
 
 // Pure helper: get the next cached ad set
 function nextFromChoiceCache(ctrl: ChoiceCache): AdResponse[] | null {
   if (ctrl.choiceCache.length === 0) return null
   const set = ctrl.choiceCache[ctrl.choiceCacheIndex % ctrl.choiceCache.length]!
   ctrl.choiceCacheIndex = (ctrl.choiceCacheIndex + 1) % ctrl.choiceCache.length
   return set
 }

+  /** Drop every queued copy of `content` (compared trimmed). Used when the
+   *  server refused that text under the usage policy: a copy would only be
+   *  refused again and count again. Returns how many were dropped. */
+  const dropQueuedCopies = useCallback(
+    (content: string): number => {
+      const refused = content.trim()
+      const current = queuedMessagesRef.current
+      const next = current.filter(
+        (message) => message.content.trim() !== refused,
+      )
+      if (next.length === current.length) return 0
+
+      writeQueue(next)
+      return current.length - next.length
+    },
+    [writeQueue],
+  )
+
   /** Move a queued message to `toIndex`, clamped to the queue's bounds so
    *  callers can pass index±1 at the edges without a guard. */
   const moveQueuedMessage = useCallback(
     (id: string, toIndex: number): boolean => {
       const current = queuedMessagesRef.current
       const from = current.findIndex((message) => message.id === id)
       if (from === -1) return false
 
       const to = Math.max(0, Math.min(current.length - 1, toIndex))
       if (to === from) return false
 
       const next = [...current]
       const [moved] = next.splice(from, 1)
       next.splice(to, 0, moved!)
       writeQueue(next)
       return true
     },
     [writeQueue],
   )

+  // already cancelled above; `getPartnerAd` keeps it, so asking again on
+  // reopen makes no second request.
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!enabled) setAd(null)
+  }, [enabled])
+
   // A late fill must not move the highlight onto the ad or another command.
   const previous = useRef({ items, menuItems, selectedIndex })
   useLayoutEffect(() => {
     const before = previous.current
     if (items === before.items && menuItems !== before.menuItems) {
       const id = before.menuItems[before.selectedIndex]?.id
       const next = menuItems.findIndex((item) => item.id === id)
       setSelectedIndex(next >= 0 ? next : Math.max(0, before.selectedIndex - 1))
     }
     previous.current = { items, menuItems, selectedIndex }
   }, [items, menuItems, selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex])
   return menuItems
 }

 // Choose the agent definition by explicit selection or mode-based fallback.
 const resolveAgent = (
   agentMode: AgentMode,
   agentId: string | undefined,
   agentDefinitions: AgentDefinition[],
 ): AgentDefinition | string => {
   const selectedAgentDefinition =
     agentId && agentDefinitions.length > 0
       ? agentDefinitions.find((definition) => definition.id === agentId)
       : undefined
 
   return selectedAgentDefinition ?? agentId ?? getAgentIdForMode(agentMode)
 }

+/**
+ * The server refused the message under the usage policy before any model saw
+ * it. Final: the same text is refused again and counts again, so the client
+ * never sends a copy of it on its own.
+ */
+export const isPromptPolicyRefusal = (error: unknown): boolean =>

+
 const getCliApiErrorDetails = (error: unknown) => {
   const parsed = extractApiErrorDetails(error)
   const topLevel = getTopLevelApiErrorDetails(error)
 
   return {
     statusCode: topLevel.statusCode ?? parsed.statusCode,
     errorCode: topLevel.errorCode ?? parsed.errorCode,
     // Prefer responseBody messages over top-level HTTP status text.
     message: parsed.message ?? topLevel.message,
     countryCode: topLevel.countryCode ?? parsed.countryCode,
     countryBlockReason:
       topLevel.countryBlockReason ?? parsed.countryBlockReason,
     ipPrivacySignals: topLevel.ipPrivacySignals ?? parsed.ipPrivacySignals,
   }
 }
 
 export const getFreebuffRateLimitErrorMessage = (
   error: unknown,
 ): string | null => {
   const details = getCliApiErrorDetails(error)
   if (details.statusCode !== 429) return null

     // Our own rate limiter's message is already user-facing and includes the
     // retry countdown — show it verbatim.
     return details.message ?? FREEBUFF_RATE_LIMIT_MESSAGE
